&\quad+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\int_{\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}}D_{j}^{\pm}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\cdot\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x+\varepsilon\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\cdot\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x-\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\int_{\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}q_{j}^{\pm}\cdot\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\\
&\quad-\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\cdot\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\cdot\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x-\varepsilon\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\cdot\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\\
&=\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\int_{\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}}f_{j}(w_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}g_{j}\left(\tfrac{w_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x-\int_{N_{\varepsilon}}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert h_{j}\left(\tfrac{w_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}(x).\end{split}

(3.39)

To solve a linearised version of (
3.39
), we may use a standard Galerkin scheme. When dealing with the initial problem for
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
, the coupling between
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
and
J_{\varepsilon}
in the time derivative would make it necessary to deal with time-dependent basis functions in the scheme, which we overcome by introducing
w_{\varepsilon}
.
The existence of a weak solution
w_{\varepsilon}
to the problem (
3.39
) where the nonlinearities are independent of the
z
-variable, that is
f,\,g,\,h\in L^{2}(0,T)^{m}
, is obtained using a Galerkin scheme, where the compactness of the approximating sequence is due to estimates similar to those in
Lemma 3.4
.
The existence of a solution
w_{\varepsilon}
to the nonlinear problem (
3.39
) then follows using Schäfer’s fixed point theorem. For this purpose, let
\beta\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
and
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta}
defined in analogy to
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
.
We consider the operator

\displaystyle{\mathcal{F}}\colon L^{2}(0,T;({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta})^{m})\to W^{1,2,2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{m},({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{m})^{\prime}),\qquad{\mathcal{F}}(\bar{w}_{\varepsilon})=w_{\varepsilon},

(3.40)

where
w_{\varepsilon}
denotes the weak solution of (
3.39
) with nonlinearities evaluated in
\bar{w}_{\varepsilon}
. By the growth conditions (
2.20
),
J_{\varepsilon}\simeq 1
and the fact that the trace space of
H^{\beta}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})
continuously embeds into
L^{2}(\partial\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})
if
\beta>\frac{1}{2}
, the operator
{\mathcal{F}}
is well-defined. Moreover, due to the continuity of the reaction rates in the last variable and by Pratt’s theorem (see
[
Pra60
]
), the evaluation of the nonlinearities is continuous with respect to
L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta})
-convergence, and hence by the compactness of the embedding
W^{1,2,2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon},{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime})\xhookrightarrow{}L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta})
(see
[
Lio69
, Theorem 5.1, p. 58]
) as well as the a priori estimates for the linear problem, we have that
{\mathcal{F}}\colon L^{2}(0,T;({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta})^{m})\to L^{2}(0,T;({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta})^{m})
is continuous and compact. Based on the a priori estimates in
Lemma 3.4
, the set

\displaystyle\left\{w_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\beta})^{m})\,\colon\,w_{\varepsilon}=\lambda{\mathcal{F}}(w_{\varepsilon})\text{ for some }\lambda\in[0,1]\right\}

(3.41)

is bounded as it only consists of weak solutions of (
3.39
) where the nonlinearities are multiplied by
\lambda
. Therefore, Schäfer’s fixed-point theorem ensures the existence of a weak solution
w_{\varepsilon}
of (
3.39
), which yields a weak solution
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}=\frac{w_{\varepsilon}}{J_{\varepsilon}}
of (
3.11
) in the sense of
Definition 3.1
.

If
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(1)}
and
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(2)}
are weak solutions of (
3.11
), by testing (
3.9
) with
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(1)}-\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(2)}
, an argument similar to that in the proof of
Lemma 3.4
, based on the Lipschitz continuity of the nonlinearities, yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}}{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t}\lVert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(1)}-\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(2)}\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon}^{m}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lesssim\lVert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(1)}-\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{(2)}\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon}^{m}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.42)

The uniqueness of the weak solution is then a consequence of Gronwall’s inequality.
∎

3.2
Estimates for the shifts of the microsolutions

In
Subsection 4.1
, we aim to establish strong two-scale convergence of the microsolutions
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}
to pass to the limit in the nonlinear reaction terms. Using
Theorem A.13
, this is achieved by controlling shifts with respect to
x^{\prime}\in\Sigma
of the functions
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}
and its gradients, for which we introduce the following terminology (see
[
GN21
]
).
For
h\in(0,1)
, we write

\displaystyle\Sigma_{h}\coloneqq\left\{x^{\prime}\in\Sigma\,\colon\,\mathrm{dist}(x^{\prime},\partial\Sigma)>h\right\}

(3.43)

but cannot work with the corresponding sets

\displaystyle\Omega_{\varepsilon,h}\coloneqq\Omega_{\varepsilon}\cap(\Sigma_{h}\times(-H,H))\qquad\text{and}\qquad\Omega_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}\coloneqq\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\cap\Omega_{\varepsilon,h},

(3.44)

as the channels may intersect with the boundary of
\Sigma_{h}\times(-H,H)
. Hence, we possibly lose the Lipschitz-regularity of
\Omega_{\varepsilon,h}
and further introduce the sets

\displaystyle\widehat{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,h}\coloneqq\operatorname{int}\left(\bigcup_{k^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,h}}\varepsilon(\overline{Y}+k^{\prime})\right)\qquad\text{and}\qquad\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}\coloneqq\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}\cap(\widehat{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,h}\times(-\varepsilon,\varepsilon)),

(3.45)

where
{\mathcal{I}}_{\varepsilon,h}=\left\{k^{\prime}\in{\mathbbm{Z}}^{n-1}\,\colon\,\varepsilon(Y+k^{\prime})\subset\Sigma_{h}\right\}
. We denote the corresponding bulk regions by

\displaystyle\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{+}\coloneqq\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{+}\cap(\widehat{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,h}\times(\varepsilon,H))\qquad\text{and}\qquad\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{-}\coloneqq\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{-}\cap(\widehat{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,h}\times(-H,-\varepsilon)),

(3.46)

the top and bottom (resp. lateral boundaries) of the channels in
\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}
by
\widehat{S}_{\varepsilon,*,h}^{\pm}
(resp.
\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}
), and we finally write

\displaystyle\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}=\operatorname{int}\left(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{+}\cup\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{-}\cup\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}\cup\widehat{S}_{\varepsilon,*,h}^{+}\cup\widehat{S}_{\varepsilon,*,h}^{-}\right).

(3.47)

Moreover, for
l^{\prime}\in{\mathbbm{Z}}^{n-1}
,
U\subset{\mathbbm{R}}^{n}
and
v_{\varepsilon}\colon(0,T)\times U\to{\mathbbm{R}}
, we denote
v_{\varepsilon}^{l^{\prime}}(t,x)\coloneqq v_{\varepsilon}(t,x+(l^{\prime},0)\varepsilon)
,
extending
v_{\varepsilon}
by zero to
{\mathbbm{R}}^{n}
, and introduce the shifts

\displaystyle\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}v_{\varepsilon}(t,x)\coloneqq v_{\varepsilon}^{l^{\prime}}(t,x)-v_{\varepsilon}(t,x).

(3.48)

As
\partial_{t}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
is only a functional on
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}\simeq H^{1}(\Omega_{\varepsilon})
, it is a priori not clear how to obtain the time derivative of
\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
. For this purpose, we further introduce the spaces

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}\coloneqq L^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{+})\times L^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})\times L^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{-}),

(3.49)

equipped with the inner product of
{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon}
, and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}\coloneqq\left\{v_{\varepsilon}\in H^{1}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h})\,\colon\,v_{\varepsilon}\rvert_{\partial\widehat{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,h}\times(-H,-\varepsilon)}=v_{\varepsilon}\rvert_{\partial\widehat{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,h}\times(\varepsilon,H)}=0\right\},

(3.50)

equipped with the inner product of
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
, using that
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}\xhookrightarrow{}{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
after extending functions defined on
\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}
by zero to
\Omega_{\varepsilon}
. Therefore, we have the Gelfand triple
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}\xhookrightarrow{}{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}\xhookrightarrow{}{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}^{\prime}
, and for every
v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon})
with
\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime})
there holds
\partial_{t}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}^{\prime})
with

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}}=\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\delta_{-l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\varphi_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}\qquad\text{for all }\varphi_{\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}.

(3.51)

Indeed, for every
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}((0,T))
and
\varphi_{\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}
, we have
\delta_{-l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\varphi_{\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
and thus

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\delta_{-l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\varphi_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}\psi(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{\varepsilon},\delta_{-l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\varphi_{\varepsilon}\right)_{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon}}\psi^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t=-\int_{0}^{T}\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}\right)_{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}}\psi^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t;

(3.52)

hence (
3.51
) follows due to the
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}
-continuity of the map
\varphi_{\varepsilon}\mapsto\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\delta_{-l,\varepsilon}\varphi_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}
and the uniqueness of the generalised time derivative.
The shifts of the microsolutions
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
are then estimated as follows.

Lemma 3.6

(Shifts of
J_{\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
)
.

For every
0<h\ll 1
and
l^{\prime}\in{\mathbbm{Z}}^{n-1}
with
|l^{\prime}\varepsilon|\ll h
, there holds

\displaystyle\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon})\rVert_{\smash{L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,2h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}(0)\tilde{U}_{j\varepsilon,0})\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rVert_{\smash{L^{2}(0,T;\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\varepsilon+|l^{\prime}\varepsilon|+|l^{\prime}\varepsilon|\varepsilon,

(3.53)

where the constant only depends on
h
,
T
and the constants from
(T3)
,
(S1)
,
(S2)
and
Lemma 3.4
.

Proof.

To shorten notation, for measurable sets
E\subset{\mathbbm{R}}^{n}
, we write
\left(\cdot,\cdot\right)_{E}
instead of
\left(\cdot,\cdot\right)_{L^{2}(E)}
and, for
t\in(0,T)
, we write
\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\cdot\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{p}(E)}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}=\lVert\cdot\rVert_{\smash{L^{p}((0,t)\times E)}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}
. Moreover,
C_{\theta,h}
always denotes a generic constant depending only on
\theta,\,h>0
. Testing the weak formulation (
3.9
) with
\delta_{-l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\varphi_{\varepsilon}
for functions
\varphi_{\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}^{m}
and taking into account (
3.51
), it is obtained that the shifts
\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
satisfy

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon})),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}}

(3.54)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(D_{j}^{\pm}\nabla\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}+\varepsilon\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\nabla\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}

(3.55)

\displaystyle\quad-\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}q_{j}^{\pm}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}-\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{u}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}

(3.56)

\displaystyle=\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(\delta f_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\delta\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}g_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})\right),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}-\left(\delta\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert h_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})\right),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}},

(3.57)

where we have defined

\displaystyle\delta f_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})\coloneqq f_{j}((\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})^{l^{\prime}})-f_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}),\qquad\delta(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}g_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}))\coloneqq J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}g_{j}((\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})^{l^{\prime}})-J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}g_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),

(3.58)

\displaystyle\delta\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert h_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})\right)\coloneqq J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert^{l^{\prime}}h_{j}((\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})^{l^{\prime}})-J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert h_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}).

(3.59)

To derive the desired estimates, we write
w_{\varepsilon}=J_{\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
, use
J_{\varepsilon}\nabla u_{\varepsilon}=\nabla w_{\varepsilon}-\frac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}}{J_{\varepsilon}}w_{\varepsilon}
and
\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}a_{\varepsilon}b_{\varepsilon}=a_{\varepsilon}^{l^{\prime}}(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}b_{\varepsilon})+(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}a_{\varepsilon})b_{\varepsilon}
which holds for arbitrary real-valued functions
a_{\varepsilon}
and
b_{\varepsilon}
, so that we obtain from the previous equation after rearranging terms

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(D_{j}^{\pm,l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}+\varepsilon\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}

(3.60)

\displaystyle\quad=-\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}D_{j}^{\pm}\,\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\,\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}q_{j}^{\pm},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\,q_{j}^{\pm,l^{\prime}},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}

(3.61)

\displaystyle\qquad-\varepsilon\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\,\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}+\varepsilon\left(w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\,\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}

(3.62)

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\,\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)^{l^{\prime}},\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}+\left(w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}

(3.63)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\,(\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}-\tfrac{1}{\varepsilon}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}),\nabla\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(\delta f_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}

(3.64)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\delta\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}g_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})\right),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}-\left(\delta\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert h_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})\right),\varphi_{j\varepsilon}\right)_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}}

(3.65)

\displaystyle\quad\eqqcolon\sum_{i=1}^{3}A_{i,\varepsilon}^{\pm}+\sum_{i=1}^{5}A_{i,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}+A_{\varepsilon}^{f}+A_{\varepsilon}^{g}+A_{\varepsilon}^{h}.

(3.66)

Due to the boundary values of
J_{\varepsilon}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
, we cannot simply choose it as a test function in the previous formulation and introduce an additional cut-off function
\eta\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}(\widehat{\Sigma}_{h})
with
0\leq\eta\leq 1
,
\eta\equiv 1
in
\widehat{\Sigma}_{2h}
and
|\nabla\eta|\lesssim\frac{1}{h}
. We thus have
\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}
with

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}),\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}}{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t}\lVert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}\rVert_{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}}^{2},

(3.67)

and taking
\varphi_{\varepsilon}=\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}
as a test function above, the terms on the left-hand side are estimated by

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}),\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,h,0}}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(D_{j}^{\pm,l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm},\nabla(\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon})\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}

(3.68)

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\nabla(\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon})\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}

(3.69)

\displaystyle\quad\gtrsim\frac{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}}{\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t}\lVert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\lVert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rVert_{\smash{L^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\left(D_{j}^{\pm,l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm},\eta\nabla\eta\,\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}

(3.70)

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon\lVert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert_{\smash{L^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\varepsilon\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\eta\nabla\eta\,\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}.

(3.71)

In what follows, we repeatedly use the a priori estimates from
Lemma 3.4
, Young’s inequality for
\theta\in(0,1)
and the boundedness of the coefficients and of
F_{\varepsilon}
, to estimate the latter scalar products and the terms
A_{i,\varepsilon}^{\dagger}
,
A_{\varepsilon}^{f}
,
A_{\varepsilon}^{g}
,
A_{\varepsilon}^{h}
. For the scalar products, we conclude

\displaystyle\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left(D_{j}^{\pm,l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm},\eta\nabla\eta\,\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}\right|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau+\varepsilon\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\eta\nabla\eta\,\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}\right|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.72)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}({\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta h^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\theta h^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.73)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}({\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+C_{\theta,h}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+C_{\theta,h}\varepsilon^{2}.

(3.74)

Next, we estimate the different terms
A_{i,\varepsilon}^{\dagger}
separately. First, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{1,\varepsilon}^{\pm}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.75)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{1}{\theta}+\frac{1}{h^{2}}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}D_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.76)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+C_{\theta,h}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}D_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.77)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{2,\varepsilon}^{\pm}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.78)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{1}{\theta}+\frac{1}{h^{2}}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}q_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.79)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+C_{\theta,h}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}q_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.80)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{3,\varepsilon}^{\pm}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert q_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.81)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{h}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert q_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.82)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\theta\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{h}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.83)

The proof of the estimates for the terms on
\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}
proceeds similarly, but we have to carefully take into account the dependence on
\varepsilon
and use the assumptions
(T1)
–
(T4)
. We compute

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{1,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.84)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{\varepsilon}{\theta}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{h^{2}}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.85)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.86)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\theta,h}(1+\varepsilon^{2})\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.87)

Similarly,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{2,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.88)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{\varepsilon}{\theta}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{h^{2}}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.89)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.90)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\theta,h}\varepsilon^{2}(1+\varepsilon^{2})\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.91)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{3,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon}{\theta}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.92)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon^{3}}{h^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.93)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{h^{2}}.

(3.94)

The estimates for
A_{4,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}
and
A_{5,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}
are obtained analogously and we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{4,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.95)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon}{h^{2}}\Big)\Big(\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2},

(3.96)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.97)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\theta,h}(1+\varepsilon^{2})\Big(\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\Big)

(3.98)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{5,\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.99)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\Big(\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.100)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\varepsilon}{h^{2}}\Big(\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\Big)\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.101)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{h^{2}}.

(3.102)

It remains to give the estimates for the nonlinearities
f
,
g
, and
h
. Due to the uniform Lipschitz continuity of
f
in the last argument and using
w_{\varepsilon}=\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}
on
\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{\varepsilon}^{f}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau\leq\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm}}|(f_{j}\circ u_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})^{l^{\prime}}-f_{j}\circ u_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}|\,\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.103)

Next, we rewrite
A_{\varepsilon}^{g}
according to

\displaystyle A_{\varepsilon}^{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\delta g_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}g_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}),\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}\eqqcolon A_{1,\varepsilon}^{g}+A_{2,\varepsilon}^{g}

(3.104)

and estimate both terms separately. Due to the uniform Lipschitz continuity of
g
in the last argument, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{1,\varepsilon}^{g}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}|J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}-J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\,\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.105)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|^{2}+|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}||\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}|\,\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.106)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.107)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.108)

and, using
|\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}|,\simeq\varepsilon
, we calculate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{2,\varepsilon}^{g}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|(1+|\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}|)\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.109)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}|\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.110)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.111)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.112)

The nonlinearity involving
h
is treated in a similar way. We write

\displaystyle A_{\varepsilon}^{h}

\displaystyle=\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\delta h_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}),\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}}+\left(\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\left(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\right)h_{j}(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}),\eta^{2}\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}}

(3.113)

\displaystyle\eqqcolon A_{1,\varepsilon}^{h}+A_{2,\varepsilon}^{h}

(3.114)

and use the uniform Lipschitz continuity of
h
in the last variable together with the trace estimate in
Lemma 3.3
, which in particular yields
\lVert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert_{\smash{L^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lesssim 1
by the a priori estimates from
Lemma 3.4
. We thus obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{1,\varepsilon}^{h}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}}\left|J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\,\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}-J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\,\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right|\,\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}(x)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.115)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}}\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|^{2}+|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\,|\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}|\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}(x)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.116)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.117)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\theta\varepsilon}{h^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.118)

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.119)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{h^{2}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.120)

Moreover, using
|\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}|\simeq 1
, the term
A_{2,\varepsilon}^{h}
can be estimated by

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|A_{2,\varepsilon}^{h}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}}\left|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert)\right|(1+|\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}|)\,\eta^{2}|\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}|\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}(x)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}\tau

(3.121)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}|\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.122)

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M},l^{\prime}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.123)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\theta\varepsilon}{h^{2}}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.124)

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.125)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\theta\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{h^{2}}

(3.126)

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2},

(3.127)

where we have used

\displaystyle\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}(J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lesssim\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\lVert F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.128)

in the last step.
Altogether, writing
W_{\varepsilon,0}=J_{\varepsilon}(0)\tilde{U}_{\varepsilon,0}
, for
\theta
sufficiently small and almost every
t\in(0,T)
we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}(t)\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\nabla w_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}

(3.129)

\displaystyle\lesssim\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}{W}_{j\varepsilon,0}\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\eta\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}({\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}

(3.130)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\mathop{\smash{\sum_{\pm}}}\displaylimits\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}w_{j\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{2}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}D_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}q_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\right)

(3.131)

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\varepsilon^{2})\left(\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\right)

(3.132)

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\varepsilon^{2})\left(\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert_{\smash{L^{\infty}(0,T)\times\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}\right)

(3.133)

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}^{2}.

(3.134)

For invertible matrices
A,\,B\in{\mathbbm{R}}^{n\times n}
, we have
A^{-1}-B^{-1}=-A^{-1}(A-B)B^{-1}
and hence, by
(T1)
and
(T3)
, also
\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\rVert\lesssim|l^{\prime}\varepsilon|
. Therefore, by
(S1)
–
(S3)
, we conclude

\displaystyle\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}D_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}q_{j}^{\pm}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}_{\varepsilon,h}^{\pm})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{q}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.135)

\displaystyle\quad+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\lVert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}F_{\varepsilon}^{-\top}\nu_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{N}_{\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}

(3.136)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\tilde{b}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}+\varepsilon\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\kern-0.6458pt\lvert\delta_{l^{\prime},\varepsilon}\left(\tilde{D}_{j\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\tfrac{\nabla J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}{J_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}}\right)\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert\kern-0.6458pt\rvert_{\smash{L_{t}^{\infty}(\widehat{\Omega}^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon,h})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\lesssim|l^{\prime}\varepsilon|,

(3.137)

where the estimates hold uniformly in
t\in(0,T)
.
An application of Gronwall’s inequality concludes the proof.
∎

A further ingredient for deriving strong compactness of the sequence of microsolutions
(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger})_{\varepsilon}
, where
\dagger\in\{\pm,\,\mathrm{M}\}
, is a suitable control on the generalised time derivatives
\partial_{t}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger}
, see
Theorem A.13
. Following
[
GN21
, Section 2.2]
, we next show how they are related to the time derivative
\partial_{t}\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime})
, as it is not obvious how to restrict a functional on
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
to the subdomains
\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger}
. For this purpose, we consider the spaces

\displaystyle{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\pm}=H^{1}_{S_{*,\varepsilon}^{\pm},0}(\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})\quad\text{and}\quad{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\mathrm{M}}=H^{1}_{S_{*,\varepsilon}^{+}\cup S_{*,\varepsilon}^{-},0}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}),

(3.138)

each equipped with the scalar product of
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
by considering
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\dagger}
as a subspace of
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}
extending functions defined only on
\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}
or
\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}
to
\Omega_{\varepsilon}
by zero.
Then,
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\dagger}\xhookrightarrow{}L^{2}(\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger})\xhookrightarrow{}({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\dagger})^{\prime}
is a Gelfand triple and, for any function
v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon})
with
\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime})
, we have
\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger}\in L^{2}(0,T;{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\dagger})
with

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rclcl}\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}^{\pm},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\pm})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\pm}}&=&\!\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}&&\text{for every }\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\pm},\\
\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right\rangle_{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\mathrm{M}})^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\mathrm{M}}}&=&\!\varepsilon\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}&&\text{for every }\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\mathrm{M}}\end{array}

and, hence,

\displaystyle\lVert\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\rVert_{\smash{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\pm})^{\prime}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\leq\lVert\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\lVert\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\rVert_{\smash{({\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\mathrm{M}})^{\prime}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}\leq\varepsilon\lVert\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon}\rVert_{\smash{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime}}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}.

(3.141)

This is indeed a direct consequence of the identities

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}\psi(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon}}\psi^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t=-\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{\varepsilon}^{\pm},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\pm}\right)_{L^{2}(\Omega_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})}\psi^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t,

(3.142)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}\psi(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{{\mathcal{L}}_{\varepsilon}}\psi^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}\right)_{L^{2}(\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})}\psi^{\prime}(t)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t,

(3.143)

where
\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger}\in{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\dagger}
and
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathrm{c}}((0,T))
, such as the uniqueness of the generalised time derivative and the
{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon,0}^{\dagger}
-continuity of the maps
\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger}\mapsto\left\langle\partial_{t}v_{\varepsilon},\varphi_{\varepsilon}^{\dagger}\right\rangle_{{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}^{\prime},\,{\mathcal{H}}_{\varepsilon}}
.

4
Limit passage and identification of the macroscopic model

In this section, we use the estimates obtained in
Section 3
to pass to the limit
\varepsilon\to 0
in the microscopic model (
3.9
). In a first step, we construct a suitable limit function
\tilde{u}_{0}
. Then, we discuss the effective model satisfied by the limit function, both on the reference and the time-dependent domain.

4.1
Convergence results for the reference problem

In what follows, we gather the convergence properties of the sequences
(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\pm})_{\varepsilon}
and
(\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}})_{\varepsilon}
. Similar results were obtained in
[
NJ07
]
and
[
GN21
]
, both in case of a static microstructure; hence, we pay particular attention when dealing with the microsolutions
\tilde{u}_{\varepsilon}^{\mathrm{M}}
.

In order to keep track of the microscopic structure of the layer in the limit
\varepsilon\to 0
, we use the notion of two-scale convergence introduced in
[
Ngu89
,
All92
]
. The dimension-reduction due to the shrinking of
\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}
to the
(n-1)
-dimensional hyperplane
\Sigma
is taken into account by including prefactors in
\varepsilon
in a suitable way, see
[
GN21
,
NJ07
,
MM00
]
. We denote by
C^{0}_{\#}(\overline{Z_{*}})
the space of continuous
(Y,0)
-periodic functions on
{\mathbbm{R}}^{n-1}\times[-1,1]
restricted to
\overline{Z_{*}}
. The space
C^{0}_{\#}(N)
is defined in an analogous way.

Definition 4.1

(Two-scale convergence in thin layers)
.

Let
p,\,p^{\prime}\in[1,\infty)
such that
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}=1
.

(i)

A sequence of functions
v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{p}((0,T)\times\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})

converges in the (weak) two-scale sense (on
\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}
)
to
v_{0}\in L^{p}((0,T)\times\Sigma\times Z_{*})
if for all
\varphi\in L^{p^{\prime}}((0,T)\times\Sigma;C^{0}_{\#}(\overline{Z_{*}})))
there holds

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}}v_{\varepsilon}(t,x)\varphi\left(t,x^{\prime},\tfrac{x}{\varepsilon}\right)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma}\int_{Z_{*}}v_{0}(t,x^{\prime},z)\varphi(t,x^{\prime},z)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}z\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t.

(4.1)

The sequence
(v_{\varepsilon})_{\varepsilon}

converges in the strong two-scale sense (on
\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}
)
to
v_{0}
if, additionally,

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{p}}\lVert v_{\varepsilon}\rVert_{\smash{L^{p}((0,T)\times\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}=\lVert v_{0}\rVert_{\smash{L^{p}((0,T)\times\Sigma\times Z_{*})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}.

(4.2)

(ii)

A sequence of functions
v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{p}((0,T)\times N_{\varepsilon})

converges in the (weak) two-scale sense on
N_{\varepsilon}
to
v_{0}\in L^{p}((0,T)\times\Sigma\times N)
if for all
\varphi\in L^{p^{\prime}}(0,T;C^{0}(\overline{\Sigma};C^{0}_{\#}(\overline{N})))
there holds

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{N_{\varepsilon}}v_{\varepsilon}(t,x)\varphi\left(t,x^{\prime},\tfrac{x}{\varepsilon}\right)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}(x)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma}\int_{N}v_{0}(t,x^{\prime},z)\varphi(t,x^{\prime},z)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}(z)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t.

(4.3)

The sequence
(v_{\varepsilon})_{\varepsilon}

converges in the strong two-scale sense on
N_{\varepsilon}
to
v_{0}
if, additionally,

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}\lVert v_{\varepsilon}\rVert_{\smash{L^{p}((0,T)\times N_{\varepsilon})}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}=\lVert v_{0}\rVert_{\smash{L^{p}((0,T)\times\Sigma\times N)}\raisebox{-0.60275pt}{\mathstrut}}.

(4.4)

A notion of two-scale convergence on time-dependent bulk domains was introduced in
[
Wie22
]
, which coincides with the standard one on periodic bulk domains under suitable assumptions on the transformation
\psi_{\varepsilon}
. The next remark briefly discusses how a definition of two-scale convergence on the thin (time-dependent) layer
\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}(t)
, which is compatible with that of
Definition 4.1
, can be given in the same spirit.

Remark 4.2

(Two-scale convergence on thin (time-dependent) layers)
.

Let
G_{\varepsilon}(t)\in\{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon},\,\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{*,\varepsilon}(t)\}
and
G(t,x^{\prime})\in\{Z_{*},\,Z_{*}(t,x^{\prime})\}
, where
Z_{*}(t,x^{\prime})\coloneqq\psi_{0}^{-1}(t,x^{\prime},Z_{*})
.
Given a sequence of functions
{v_{\varepsilon}\in L^{p}(0,T;G_{\varepsilon}(t))}
and
v_{0}\in L^{p}((0,T)\times\Sigma\times Z)
, we say that
(v_{\varepsilon})_{\varepsilon}
two-scale converges to
v_{0}
if for all
\varphi\in L^{p^{\prime}}((0,T)\times\Sigma;C^{0}_{\#}(\overline{Z}))
there holds

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon\to 0}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{\varepsilon}}\bar{v}_{\varepsilon}(t,x)\varphi\left(t,x^{\prime},\tfrac{x}{\varepsilon}\right)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma}\int_{Z}v_{0}(t,x^{\prime},z)\varphi(t,x^{\prime},z)\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}z\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\mathop{}\!\mathrm{d}t,

(4.5)

where
\bar{v}_{\varepsilon}
denotes the extension of
v_{\varepsilon}
to
\Omega^{\mathrm{M}}_{\varepsilon}
by zero. By a suitable choice of test functions, we may then consider
v_{0}
as a function in
L^{p}((0,T)\times\Sigma;G(t,x^{\prime}))
